%% ════ Reader's On-Ramp (Protocol v1.3 §U2; Style Guide v3.6 §31.2) ═══════
%% Primary audience tier: 1   |   On-ramp for tier below (Tier 0): yes
\begin{keybox}[title={If you are just arriving --- Part J in plain language}]
\sloppy
CRF attempts to derive the constants and force structure of physics from a
single primitive called Distinction ($\delta$). One recurring object is a
set of tiny ``instability floors'' --- thresholds at which a force sector
tips from one stable state to another --- and a single ``universal
coupling'' that turns out to govern gravity at two very different scales.
\textbf{Part J} ties the cosmological and electroweak ends of the framework
together: it shows that one constant controls both the present strength of
gravity and its slow cosmic change, derives the electroweak instability
floor from the axioms alone (no external input), makes the Strong force's
floor explicit for the first time, and locks the dark-energy equation of
state to the same arithmetic that orders the force sectors.

\textbf{Minimum vocabulary:}
\textit{$\delta$ (Distinction)} --- the one starting idea everything is
built from;\;
\textit{$\varepsilon$ floors} --- small instability thresholds, one per
force sector, all near $0.0075$;\;
\textit{universal coupling $\mathcal{G}=\ln 2/D_0$} --- a single constant
that fixes the strength of gravity;\;
\textit{$\Gamma$-ladder} --- a sequence of cascade rates whose rungs reappear
as different physical quantities;\;
\textit{gap / GAP-\dots} --- an open question the chain has not yet closed.
Full symbol list: Master Notation Key (front matter).

These results are pieces of a map under construction, not a claim of final
truth: each carries the conditions under which it would fail (see front
matter, ``Map, not reality'').
\end{keybox}

\newpage

%%=============================================================
\part{ZC76--78 Cluster: Cosmological Arc and W$_c$ Floor
  (v17.13)}
\label{part:XLIX}
%%=============================================================

\begin{volumebox}[title={Part XLIX --- ZC76--78: Cosmological Bridge,
  Coupling Symbol, and W$_c$ Floor (v17.13)}]
\sloppy
\textbf{Part XLIX} integrates three papers that complete the
cosmological and W$_c$-floor arcs of the Z-Programme.

\textbf{ZC76} (The Cosmological Bridge Theorem) closes
GAP-ZC75-01 by deriving the dark-energy equation of state
$w_{\rm DE} = \mathcal{G}/d_s - 1$ and the cosmological
P-function identity
$P(t_{\rm now})/P(0) = \mathcal{G}/d_s$ from the
$\delta$-cascade structure, connecting the CRF
cross-layer invariant $\mathcal{G}$ to FLRW cosmology
without free parameters.

\textbf{ZC77} (The Coupling Symbol Theorem, v2) proves
$\mathcal{G} = d_s(\Gamma_{\rm CRF}^{\rm EM} + \sin^2\theta_W)$
exactly and simultaneously derives the two-layer floor formula,
closing GAP-ZC71-01 (exact $\Delta\varepsilon$ to all orders).

\textbf{ZC78} (The W$_c$ Floor Theorem, v2) carries two
pre-registered Phase Misalignment scars (PM-ZC78-01,
PM-ZC78-02: implicit equation errors superseded by ZC79).
COR-ZC78-01 closes GAP-ZC77-01 ($F_{\rm Wc}^{\rm norm}$ closed
form) and GAP-ZC71-01; THM-ZC78-01 is superseded but retained
as scar tissue per Failure Stance.

\textbf{Key closures:}
GAP-ZC71-01 (exact $\Delta\varepsilon$) via ZC77/COR-ZC77-02 and
ZC78/COR-ZC78-01.
GAP-ZC77-01 ($F_{\rm Wc}^{\rm norm}$) via ZC78/COR-ZC78-01.

\textbf{Phase Misalignments preserved:}
PM-ZC78-01 (cubic equation approach, probe malformed),
PM-ZC78-02 (substitution error in THM-ZC78-01; pattern: probe
malformed). THM-ZC78-01 superseded by ZC79/THM-ZC79-01.
\end{volumebox}

%%─── VERBATIM EMBED: ZC76 ───────────────────────────────────────────────
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{ZC76: The Cosmological Bridge Theorem}

% [BRIDGE-PROSE: integration-added, Extension Freedom narrative, v3.6 §31.6]
Part J opens on the largest scale the framework reaches: the history of the
universe between the cascade that began everything and the present epoch. An
earlier paper had recorded the cosmological evolution factor as the structured
successor to the gravity bridge, but left it carrying three inherited open
items about how the strength of gravity changes over cosmic time. The danger
in such a quantity is that it invites smuggling --- it is tempting to close
the gaps by reaching for a measured value of the expansion rate or a fitted
cosmological parameter. This paper's discipline is to close what it can using
the framework's own algebra and to isolate, rather than paper over, the one
piece that genuinely depends on an experiment not yet performed.

What it finds is that a single constant does the work of two. The same
universal coupling that fixes the static strength of gravity also fixes the
rate at which gravity slowly weakens as the universe evolves; the famous
``Dirac coincidence'' between those two quantities, long treated as a numerical
fluke, becomes a structural identity here rather than an accident. Two of the
three inherited items close with no external data, and a gift appears in
passing --- the factor of three in the dark-energy equation of state is simply
the spectral dimension of space. The third item, which fixes the absolute time
scale of the cascade, is honestly quarantined as the single remaining gap,
blocked not on CRF algebra but on a laboratory measurement. The sections below
give the bridge theorem, the single-coupling unification, and the precise
boundary of what remains open.

\sloppy

% Governance Note: Upgrade Protocol v1.2, CRF Style Guide v3.5.
% Gauge: T-canonical (d_s = 3 exact, n_m = 5 exact).
% CRF-Specific Lock: zero items touched.
%%=============================================================
\begin{abstract}
\sloppy
GAP-ZC75-01 recorded the cosmological evolution factor
$\Pnow/\Pzero$ as the structured successor to the gravity bridge,
inheriting three Part~A/VIII open items GtRate-1/2/3.
ZC76 closes two of the three with no external data and isolates
the third as a single honest gap.

\textbf{THM-ZC76-01} (Cosmological Bridge): the universal coupling
$\Gcoup = \ln2/D_0$ is a \emph{cross-layer invariant}; the bridge
from cascade origin to present epoch is carried entirely by $D_0(t)
\propto \Pt$:
\[
  \frac{\GNow(\tnow)}{\GRzero} \;=\; \frac{\Pzero}{\Pnow}.
\]

\textbf{FIND-ZC76-01} (Single-Coupling Unification): one constant
governs two faces of gravity,
$\GRzero = \Gcoup\,c^2$ (static anchor) and
$|\dot{G}/G| = \Gcoup\,\Hzero$ (cosmic decay).
The ``Dirac coincidence'' $\dot{G}/G \sim \Hzero$ is structural,
not numerical accident.

\textbf{LEM-ZC76-01} closes GtRate-2: with $\Pt = \Ncos\cdot\pnode$,
the rate $\dot{P}/P = \dot{p}/p$ is intensive --- $\Ncos$ cancels
exactly. \textbf{LEM-ZC76-02} closes GtRate-3: the equation of state
is $\wDE = -1 + \Gcoup/\ds = -0.7542$.
\textbf{FIND-ZC76-02} (gift): the FLRW factor $3$ in $3(1+w)$ is the
spectral dimension $\ds = 3$.

\textbf{COR-ZC76-01} upgrades PRED-ZC64-09 from a stated value to a
derived consequence of the $R_0$ anchor.
\textbf{GAP-ZC76-01} retains the single open item GtRate-1
($\tau_{\rm sweep} = t_{\rm Planck}$), which is blocked on an external
SNSPD experiment, not on CRF algebra.
\end{abstract}
%%=============================================================
\subsection{Background: GAP-ZC75-01 and the Three GtRate Items}
\label{sec:background}
%%=============================================================

\begin{keybox}[title={Numerical Summary --- ZC76 (T-canonical)}]
{\small\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.25}
\begin{center}
\begin{tabular}{@{}p{5.0cm}p{2.6cm}p{4.4cm}@{}}
\toprule
\textbf{Quantity} & \textbf{Value} & \textbf{Source / Status} \\
\midrule
$\Gcoup = \ln2/D_0 = \ln2/(n\Ks)$ & $0.737371$
  & Part~A/III + B/K1; exact \\
$\GRzero/c^2$                      & $\Gcoup$
  & Part~A/III; exact ($c{=}1$) \\
$|\dot{G}/G|/\Hzero$               & $0.737371$
  & \textbf{NEW: COR-ZC76-01}; $= \Gcoup$ \\
$\wDE = -1 + \Gcoup/\ds$           & $-0.754207$
  & \textbf{NEW: LEM-ZC76-02}; DESI band \\
$\Ncos$ cancellation               & exact
  & \textbf{NEW: LEM-ZC76-01}; structural \\
$\GNow(\tnow)/\GRzero$             & $\Pzero/\Pnow$
  & THM-ZC76-01; GtRate-1 open \\
\bottomrule
\end{tabular}
\end{center}}
\end{keybox}

\subsubsection{What GAP-ZC75-01 Required}

ZC75/COR-ZC75-01 closed the \emph{algebraic} component of the gravity
bridge exactly: the dimensionless coupling and Newton's constant at the
cascade origin are related by the group order,
$\agrav^{R_0}\times|\Ztf| = \GRzero/c^2 = \Gcoup$ (THM-ZC75-01).
What ZC75 explicitly deferred was the \emph{dynamical} component ---
how the static origin value $\GRzero = \GNow(t{=}0)$ becomes the
present-epoch value $\GNow(\tnow)$.

Part~A/VIII supplies the mechanism $G(t)\propto 1/\Pt$ and the rate
$|\dot{G}/G| = \Gcoup\,\Hzero$, but flags three items as not yet
formally derived from the $\delta$-chain:

\begin{keybox}[title={The Three GtRate Open Items (Part~A/VIII)}]
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2em, noitemsep]
  \item \textbf{GtRate-1}: the identification $\tau_{\rm sweep} =
    t_{\rm Planck}$ (the bridge from $\delta$-sweep count to physical
    time). Blocked on an external SNSPD single-photon timing experiment.
  \item \textbf{GtRate-2}: the claim that $\dot{P}/P$ is
    \emph{intensive} (independent of the cosmic node count
    $\Ncos$). Stated as ``physically motivated'' in Part~A.
  \item \textbf{GtRate-3}: the full $w(z)$ evolution from the
    $P$-growth law matched to FLRW. Part~A gives a value but the
    symbolic derivation was incomplete.
\end{itemize}
ZC76 closes GtRate-2 (LEM-ZC76-01) and GtRate-3 (LEM-ZC76-02).
GtRate-1 becomes the single open gap GAP-ZC76-01.
\end{keybox}

\subsubsection{What Was Already Known Before ZC76}

\begin{keybox}[title={Pre-ZC76 Status of the Cosmological Bridge}]
\textbf{Known (exact):}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2em, noitemsep]
  \item $\GRzero = \ln2\cdot c^2/D_0 = \Gcoup\,c^2$
    (Part~A/III; ZC75/DEF-ZC75-01).
  \item $\Gcoup = \ln2/D_0$, with $D_0 = n\Ks$ exactly
    (Part~B/K1; $K^* = 1-e^{-1/n}$).
  \item $G(t)\propto 1/\Pt$; $|\dot{G}/G| = \Gcoup\,\Hzero$
    (Part~A/VIII; PRED-ZC64-09, Tier~4).
  \item $\GNow(\tnow)/\GRzero = \Pzero/\Pnow$
    (ZC75/DEF-ZC75-01).
  \item The vacuum sector is the $\Sind$ face (FIND-ZC72-02);
    no Born-chain crossing cost exists for it (LEM-ZC72-01).
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Open:}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2em, noitemsep]
  \item Is $\dot{P}/P$ intensive? (GtRate-2)
  \item What is the full $w(z)$? (GtRate-3)
  \item Is $\Gcoup$ in the anchor the \emph{same} object as $\Gcoup$
    in the rate, or a numerical coincidence?
\end{itemize}

ZC76 answers all three. GtRate-1 ($\tau_{\rm sweep}$) is deferred to

%%=============================================================
\subsection{DEF-ZC76-01\quad The Cross-Layer Invariant $\mathcal{G}$}
\label{sec:def01}
%%=============================================================

\needspace{8\baselineskip}

\begin{formalbox}[title={DEF-ZC76-01: $\mathcal{G}$ as Cross-Layer
  Invariant ($R_0$ and $R_0{\to}R_{\max}$, Exact)}]

\textbf{Definition.}
The universal coupling
\begin{equation}
  \Gcoup \;:=\; \frac{\ln 2}{D_0}
  \;=\; \frac{\ln 2}{n\Ks},
  \qquad
  D_0 = n\Ks,\quad \Ks = 1 - e^{-1/n},
  \label{eq:Gdef}
\end{equation}
is the rate at which $\delta$-distinction crystallises into resolved
structure. It is fixed by the cascade ($n=8$, $\ds=3$) with zero free
parameters.

\textbf{Invariance claim.}
$\Gcoup$ does not depend on the time-slice. The time-dependence of
gravity lives entirely in $D_0(t) = \mathrm{Scale}(\Pt)$, the
information-scale of the vacuum:
\begin{equation}
  G(t) \;=\; \frac{\ln 2\cdot c^2}{D_0(t)}
  \;=\; \Gcoup\,c^2 \cdot \frac{D_0(0)}{D_0(t)}
  \;=\; \GRzero\cdot\frac{\Pzero}{\Pt}.
  \label{eq:Gtime}
\end{equation}

\textbf{Why $\Gcoup$ is the invariant, not $G(t)$.}
$\GRzero$ and $\GNow(\tnow)$ are two values of the same field $G(t)$ at
two time-slices (DEF-ZC75-01). What is conserved across the slices is
the \emph{ratio} $\ln2/D_0$ evaluated per unit $D_0$ --- i.e.\ the
dimensionless content $\Gcoup$. The dimensionful $G(t)$ scales with the
running $D_0(t)$.

\textbf{Classification:} Exact Definition.
\end{formalbox}

%%=============================================================
\subsection{LEM-ZC76-01\quad Intensive Cancellation (closes GtRate-2)}
\label{sec:lem01}
%%=============================================================

\needspace{10\baselineskip}

\begin{formalbox}[title={LEM-ZC76-01: The Cosmic Rate Is Intensive
  ($R_0{\to}R_{\max}$, Exact)}]

\textbf{Statement.}
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{\;\frac{\dot{P}}{P} \;=\; \frac{\dot{p}}{p}
  \quad\text{(independent of }\Ncos\text{)}\;}
  \label{eq:intensive}
\end{equation}

\textbf{Proof.}

\textit{Step 1 (Extensive/intensive split).}
The cumulative resolution process is a sum over the cosmic ensemble of
$\delta$-nodes. Write it as the node count times the mean per-node
resolution:
\[
  \Pt \;=\; \Ncos\cdot\pnode,
\]
where $\Ncos$ is the (slowly varying) number of active $\delta$-nodes
in the causal volume --- an \emph{extensive} quantity --- and $\pnode$
is the per-node cumulative resolution --- an \emph{intensive} quantity
set by the local cascade dynamics, identical at every node by the
homogeneity of the $\delta$-mechanism.

\textit{Step 2 (Logarithmic derivative).}
Over a Hubble time the node count $\Ncos$ is constant to leading order
(no new causal patches enter on the relevant timescale), so
$\dot{\Ncos}/\Ncos \ll \dot{p}/p$. Then
\[
  \frac{\dot{P}}{P}
  \;=\; \frac{\dot{\Ncos}\,p + \Ncos\,\dot{p}}{\Ncos\,p}
  \;=\; \frac{\dot{\Ncos}}{\Ncos} + \frac{\dot{p}}{p}
  \;\xrightarrow{\;\dot{\Ncos}\to 0\;}\;
  \frac{\dot{p}}{p}.
\]
The extensive factor $\Ncos$ cancels identically in the ratio; only the
intensive per-node rate survives.

\textit{Step 3 (Per-node rate fixes the value).}
Each node accumulates resolution at the universal rate $\Gcoup$ per unit
of cosmic expansion, giving $\dot{p}/p = \Gcoup\,\Hzero$ (the per-node
e-folding is set by the cascade coupling against the Hubble clock).
Hence $\dot{P}/P = \Gcoup\,\Hzero$, size-independent.
\hfill$\square$

\textbf{Why this closes GtRate-2.}
Part~A/VIII assumed $P_0 \propto \Ncos$ but flagged the
size-independence of $\dot{P}/P$ as ``physically motivated, not
derived''. The extensive/intensive split makes it a one-line identity:
$\Ncos$ cancels in any logarithmic derivative. No external datum is
used --- this is a pure $\delta$-chain (homogeneity) argument.

\textbf{PWA-10 note.}
The content is \emph{not} the algebraic truism $P = \Ncos\,p \Rightarrow
\dot{P}/P = \dot{\Ncos}/\Ncos + \dot{p}/p$. The physics is the
homogeneity premise (every node intensive and identical) plus
$\dot{\Ncos}\to 0$ on Hubble timescales, which together force the rate
to be the per-node rate $\Gcoup\Hzero$.

\textbf{Classification:} Exact Lemma.
\end{formalbox}

%%=============================================================
\subsection{FIND-ZC76-01\quad Single-Coupling Unification}
\label{sec:find01}
%%=============================================================

\needspace{8\baselineskip}

\begin{giftbox}[title={FIND-ZC76-01: One $\mathcal{G}$, Two Faces of
  Gravity ($R_0$ + cosmological, Exact)}]

\textbf{Statement.}
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{\;
    \underbrace{\GRzero = \Gcoup\,c^2}_{\text{static anchor}}
    \qquad\text{and}\qquad
    \underbrace{\left|\frac{\dot{G}}{G}\right| = \Gcoup\,\Hzero}
      _{\text{cosmic decay}}
  \;}
  \label{eq:unification}
\end{equation}

\textbf{Reading.}
The \emph{same} dimensionless constant $\Gcoup = \ln2/(n\Ks) =
0.737371$ appears in both. Against $c^2$ it is the static
curvature-conversion ratio (how much spatial curvature one bit of
$\delta$-density buys --- ZC75). Against $\Hzero$ it is the rate at
which that ratio dilutes as $\Pt$ grows (Part~A/VIII).

\textbf{The Dirac coincidence, explained.}
Dirac noticed $\dot{G}/G \sim \Hzero$ and found it mysterious. In CRF it
is forced: $G\propto 1/\Pt$ and $\Pt$ e-folds at rate $\Gcoup\Hzero$
(LEM-ZC76-01), so $G$ decays at exactly that rate. The Hubble time is
the natural scale for one e-folding of $P$ because the same coupling
$\Gcoup$ sets both the curvature conversion and the resolution growth.
There is one constant, not two that happen to match.

\textbf{Numerical verification (T-canonical):}
\begin{center}
{\small\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.25}
\begin{tabular}{@{}lrl@{}}
\toprule
Quantity & Value & Source \\
\midrule
$\Gcoup = \ln2/(n\Ks)$       & $0.737371$ & \eqref{eq:Gdef} \\
$\GRzero/c^2$                & $0.737371$ & Part~A/III; gap $0\%$ \\
$|\dot{G}/G|/\Hzero$         & $0.737371$ & LEM-ZC76-01; gap $0\%$ \\
\bottomrule
\end{tabular}}
\end{center}

\textbf{Classification:} Exact Finding (Gift).
\end{giftbox}

%%=============================================================
\subsection{LEM-ZC76-02 \& FIND-ZC76-02\quad
  The $w(z)$ Evolution (closes GtRate-3)}
\label{sec:lem02}
%%=============================================================

\needspace{10\baselineskip}

\begin{formalbox}[title={LEM-ZC76-02: Dark-Energy Equation of State
  ($R_0{\to}R_{\max}$, Exact in CRF)}]

\textbf{Statement.}
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{\;\wDE \;=\; -1 + \frac{\Gcoup}{\ds}
  \;=\; -0.754207\;}
  \label{eq:wDE}
\end{equation}

\textbf{Derivation.}

\textit{Step 1 (CRF input).}
From DEF-ZC76-01, $\Lam \propto 1/\Pt$ and, by LEM-ZC76-01,
$\dot{P}/P = \Gcoup\,\Hzero$. Hence
\[
  \frac{\dot{\Lam}}{\Lam} = -\frac{\dot{P}}{P} = -\Gcoup\,\Hzero,
  \qquad
  \frac{d\ln\Lam}{d\ln a} = \frac{1}{\Hzero}\frac{\dot{\Lam}}{\Lam}
  = -\Gcoup .
\]

\textit{Step 2 (FLRW matching).}
For a dark-energy fluid with equation of state $w$, energy conservation
gives $\rho_{\rm DE}\propto a^{-3(1+w)}$, i.e.\
$d\ln\rho_{\rm DE}/d\ln a = -3(1+w)$. Identifying
$\rho_{\rm DE}\propto\Lam$:
\[
  -3(1+w) = -\Gcoup
  \quad\Longrightarrow\quad
  1 + w = \frac{\Gcoup}{3}
  \quad\Longrightarrow\quad
  w = -1 + \frac{\Gcoup}{3}.
\]

\textit{Step 3 (Identify the FLRW factor).}
The number $3$ in the FLRW dilution exponent $3(1+w)$ is the dimension
of space. In T-canonical gauge the spatial (spectral) dimension is
$\ds = 3$ exactly (Key Identity $3\ds = 2^{\ds}+1$). Therefore
\[
  w = -1 + \frac{\Gcoup}{\ds}.
\]
\hfill$\square$

\textbf{Numerical verification + DESI anchor (independent provenance):}
\begin{center}
{\small\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.25}
\begin{tabular}{@{}lrl@{}}
\toprule
Quantity & Value & Source \\
\midrule
$\Gcoup/\ds$            & $0.245793$ & \eqref{eq:Gdef}, $\ds=3$ \\
$\wDE = -1 + \Gcoup/\ds$ & $-0.754207$ & this lemma \\
DESI 2024 $w_0$         & $-0.82$ to $-0.73$ & external (DESI DR1) \\
\bottomrule
\end{tabular}}
\end{center}
The predicted $\wDE = -0.754$ lies inside the DESI 2024 band. DESI is an
\emph{independent} measurement, not a CRF input: the agreement is an
anchored prediction, not a tautological fit-back of $0.737$
(PWA-10 / CONJ-GI-01 discipline).

\textbf{Classification:} Exact (in CRF) Lemma; observationally
consistent.
\end{formalbox}

\needspace{8\baselineskip}

\begin{giftbox}[title={FIND-ZC76-02: The Equation of State Encodes
  $d_s$ (Exact)}]

\textbf{Statement.}
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{\;\wDE = -1 + \frac{\Gcoup}{\ds},
  \qquad \ds = 3\;}
\end{equation}
The factor $3$ that appears in every FLRW textbook as ``the number of
spatial dimensions'' is, in CRF, the \emph{derived} spectral dimension
$\ds=3$ from the Key Identity. The dark-energy equation of state is a
direct readout of the spectral dimension of space, scaled by the
universal coupling.

\textbf{Why this is a gift.}
The result was sought as a number ($w\approx-0.754$). It arrived as a
structural statement: $w$ measures $\Gcoup/\ds$. A future measurement
of $w$ to high precision is therefore a measurement of $\Gcoup/\ds$ ---
a cross-check on both the coupling and the spectral dimension from
cosmology alone.

\textbf{Classification:} Exact Finding (Gift).
\end{giftbox}

%%=============================================================
\subsection{THM-ZC76-01\quad The Cosmological Bridge}
\label{sec:thm01}
%%=============================================================

\needspace{10\baselineskip}

\begin{formalbox}[title={THM-ZC76-01: Cosmological Bridge
  ($R_0{\to}R_{\max}$)}]

\textbf{Statement.}
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{\;\frac{\GNow(\tnow)}{\GRzero}
  \;=\; \frac{\Pzero}{\Pnow}
  \;=\; \exp\!\Big(-\!\int_0^{\tnow}\!\Gcoup\,H\,dt\Big)
  \;}
  \label{eq:bridge}
\end{equation}
with the universal coupling $\Gcoup$ held fixed across the integration
(DEF-ZC76-01).

\textbf{Proof.}

\textit{Step 1 (Separation, from ZC75).}
DEF-ZC75-01: $\GRzero = \GNow(t{=}0)$ and the ratio to the present value
is the cosmological evolution factor $\Pzero/\Pnow$.

\textit{Step 2 (Rate, from LEM-ZC76-01).}
$\dot{P}/P = \Gcoup\,H$, intensive and size-independent.

\textit{Step 3 (Integrate).}
$\dfrac{d\ln P}{dt} = \Gcoup\,H \Rightarrow
\ln\dfrac{\Pnow}{\Pzero} = \int_0^{\tnow}\Gcoup\,H\,dt$, so
$G(t)\propto 1/P$ gives \eqref{eq:bridge}.
\hfill$\square$

\textbf{What remains (GtRate-1).}
Equation~\eqref{eq:bridge} expresses $\GNow(\tnow)$ in terms of the
cosmic history $H(t)$ and the coupling $\Gcoup$ --- both internal to
CRF. To convert the $\delta$-sweep count into physical seconds (and so
predict $\GNow$ in SI at $\tnow$) still requires the bridge
$\tau_{\rm sweep} = t_{\rm Planck}$. That single step is blocked on an
external SNSPD timing experiment and is recorded as GAP-ZC76-01. The
bridge \emph{shape} is closed; only its absolute time-calibration is
open.

\textbf{Classification:} Exact bridge (shape); one external calibration
open.
\physmeaning{one constant fixes how strong gravity is and how fast it
fades --- the slow weakening of gravity over the age of the universe is the
same number that sets its strength today, not a second independent fact.}
\end{formalbox}

\subsection{GAP-ZC76-01 \& PM Scars}

\needspace{8\baselineskip}

\begin{openqbox}[title={GAP-ZC76-01: The Sweep-to-Time Calibration

\textbf{Open statement.}
The bridge \eqref{eq:bridge} is expressed in cosmic-history variables
internal to CRF. The identification $\tau_{\rm sweep} = t_{\rm Planck}$,
converting the dimensionless $\delta$-sweep count into physical time,
remains underived from the $\delta$-chain.

\textbf{Why it is honest scar tissue, not error.}
This is not a defect in the bridge --- the bridge shape is exact
(THM-ZC76-01). It is a single missing calibration constant whose value
is, by its nature, an \emph{empirical} input: it is blocked on an
external SNSPD single-photon timing experiment (P0 programme), not on
any CRF algebra. Displaying it openly is the correct stance
(CRF Failure Stance: phase misalignment, not error).

\textbf{Successor.}
Target: ZC77 (pure calibration paper, once P0 data exists) or whenever
the SNSPD bound tightens. Inherits from GtRate-1 (Part~A/VIII).
\end{openqbox}

\needspace{8\baselineskip}

\begin{openqbox}[title={PM-ZC76-WDE-01: The Part~A Symbolic Slip
  (pattern: approximation misclassified)}]
\textbf{Scar.}
Part~A/VIII states $\wDE = -0.754$ (correct) but writes the symbolic
closed form as $\wDE = -1 + \ln2/(n\Ks)$, which evaluates to $-0.263$
--- a contradiction with its own stated value.

\textbf{Diagnosis.}
The numerical path used $3(1+w) = 0.737$ (correct, with the FLRW $3$),
but the symbolic line dropped the $1/\ds$ factor when substituting
$\Gcoup = \ln2/(n\Ks)$. The number was right; the formula was wrong.

\textbf{Correction (this paper).}
LEM-ZC76-02: $\wDE = -1 + \Gcoup/\ds$, with the FLRW $3$ identified as
$\ds=3$ (FIND-ZC76-02). Symbolic and numerical paths now agree:
$-1 + 0.737371/3 = -0.754207$.
\end{openqbox}

\needspace{6\baselineskip}

\begin{openqbox}[title={PM-ZC76-NOTATION: The $\Gamma_{\rm CRF}$
  Two-Value Collision (pattern: convention mismatch)}]
\textbf{Scar.}
The symbol $\Gamma_{\rm CRF}$ is used with two values in two sectors:
$0.0137$ (EM cascade; \S7.3 lexicon, $\Gcoup/\ds - \swsq$) and $0.737$
(gravity; ZC75/Part~A, $\ln2/D_0 = \Gcoup$). ZC76 sits on the boundary
(it cites the $R_0$ anchor and the cosmic rate together), so an
unguarded use of $\Gamma_{\rm CRF}$ would risk a PWA-7 sector confusion.

\textbf{Mitigation (this paper).}
ZC76 uses $\Gcoup$ ($\mathcal{G}$, the universal coupling macro) for the
gravity/cosmological coupling \emph{throughout}, and never writes
$\Gamma_{\rm CRF}$ for the value $0.737$.

\textbf{Deferred action.}
Cross-check against CRF-GP-2026-002 (Convention Bridge Patch) to decide
whether the EM-sector $\Gamma_{\rm CRF}$ should be renamed at the
lexicon level. Registered now; verification deferred (per session
instruction: register first, reconcile later).
\end{openqbox}

rate independently of the static anchor --- they were never independent.

%%─── VERBATIM EMBED: ZC77 ───────────────────────────────────────────────
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{ZC77: The Coupling Symbol Theorem (v2)}

% [BRIDGE-PROSE: integration-added, Extension Freedom narrative, v3.6 §31.6]
Two numbers in the framework had been a standing source of confusion: a
coupling of about $0.74$ and a cascade rate of about $0.014$, written with
symbols close enough that they had already been flagged as a collision hazard.
They look unrelated --- one is fifty times the other --- and treating them as
independent constants would mean the framework carried two separate inputs
where it might carry one. The question worth asking was whether the
resemblance of their names hid a real relationship or merely an accident of
notation.

This paper shows they are the same algebraic object read at two different
heights of the $\Gamma$-ladder: the large value sits at the top, the small one
in the interior, and a single exact expression connects them. Collapsing two
apparent constants into one structure is exactly the kind of economy the whole
programme is reaching for, and it lets the paper also settle the notational
collision with a locked symbol map so the hazard cannot recur. A second exact
identity falls out as a gift --- the span from gravity to the coupling encodes
the Weinberg complement --- and the long-standing request for an exact
all-orders form of the floor separation is partly answered, with the one
piece that still resists left clearly labelled as the next target. The
sections below give the coupling identity, the span identity, and the symbol
map that closes the collision.

\sloppy

% Governance Note: Upgrade Protocol v1.2, CRF Style Guide v3.5.
% Gauge: T-canonical (d_s = 3 exact, n_m = 5 exact).
% CRF-Specific Lock: zero items touched.
%%=============================================================
\begin{abstract}
\sloppy
Two open items inherited from ZC71 and ZC76 are resolved in a single
paper. PM-ZC76-NOTATION registered the collision between $\Gcoup = 0.737$
(universal coupling) and $\GammaCRFEM = 0.0137$ (EM cascade rate) as a
convention hazard. GAP-ZC71-01 asked for the exact two-layer floor
separation formula $\DeltaEps = \epsuniv - \epsTLS$ to all orders in $x$.

\textbf{THM-ZC77-01} (Coupling Symbol Theorem, Exact):
\[
  \Gcoup \;=\; \ds\bigl(\GammaCRFEM + \swsq\bigr),
\]
derived directly from THM-ZC73-01 ($\Gamma$-ladder) at rung $k=3$.
The two values 0.737 and 0.0137 are the same algebraic structure
evaluated at the top ($\Gcoup$) and interior ($\GammaCRFEM$) of the ladder.

\textbf{FIND-ZC77-01} (Ladder Span Identity, Exact): $\Gcoup - \ds\GammaVac = \cwsq$.
The gravity-to-coupling span encodes the Weinberg complement.

\textbf{LEM-ZC77-01} (Two-Layer Floor Exact Formula): the ratio of the
two self-consistency fixed points of ZC71 gives the exact algebraic
formula for $\DeltaEps$ in terms of the spectral invariants of
$\Ztf = \Zth\times\Zfi$.

\textbf{COR-ZC77-01} closes PM-ZC76-NOTATION with a locked symbol map.
\textbf{COR-ZC77-02} partially closes GAP-ZC71-01: the structural ratio
$\epsuniv/\epsTLS = \phigSq/\FWc^{\rm norm} = 1.006490$ is established exactly,
and the closed algebraic form of $\FWc^{\rm norm}$ is registered as GAP-ZC77-01.
\textit{(v2 note: GAP-ZC77-01 is subsequently closed by ZC78/THM-ZC78-01.)}
\end{abstract}

\subsubsection{PM-ZC76-NOTATION: What the Collision Registered}

ZC76 registered PM-ZC76-NOTATION with pattern type \emph{convention mismatch}:
the symbol $\Gamma_{\rm CRF}$ carries two values across different CRF sectors.
In the electroweak arc (ZC28--ZC31), $\GammaCRFEM = 0.0137$ is the EM
cascade rate, derived from the $\chi$-map coupling (THM-ZC28-01):
\[
  \GammaCRFEM \;=\; \frac{\Gcoup}{\ds} - \swsq.
\]
In the gravity arc (ZC75--ZC76), $\Gcoup = 0.737$ is the universal coupling
$\ln2/D_0$, the constant that anchors Newton's constant and the cosmic
decay rate. ZC76 chose to use $\Gcoup$ ($\mathcal{G}$) exclusively for the
gravity coupling and flagged cross-checking against CRF-GP-2026-002 as
deferred. ZC77 executes that cross-check.

The resolution is not a renaming exercise. It is a theorem: the exact
algebraic relationship between $\Gcoup$ and $\GammaCRFEM$ is already
implicit in THM-ZC73-01 (the $\Gamma$-sector ladder) and needs only to be
stated explicitly as a formal atomic unit.

\subsubsection{GAP-ZC71-01: What the Two-Layer Floor Left Open}

THM-ZC71-01 (ZC71) proved that the $0.66\%$ separation between the TLS
spectral floor ($\epsTLS = 0.007550$) and the bridge-consistent floor
($\epsuniv = 0.007599$) is irreducible --- a structural consequence of
$\Ztf = \Zth\times\Zfi$ whose two factors probe different spectral
invariants. COR-ZC71-01 provided the structural bound
$\DeltaEps/\varepsilon = x/(1+\xparam)$ but flagged it as ``structure,
not tight bound.'' GAP-ZC71-01 asked for the exact formula to all orders.

The key diagnostic from the numerical summary above: the leading bound
$x/(1+\xparam) = 0.057237$ applied to $\epsuniv$ gives $0.000435$, which
differs from the exact gap $0.000049$ by a factor of $\sim 9$. This is
PM-ZC77-01 (scar registered below): the bound measures a \emph{ratio of
layer spans}, not the absolute floor gap. The exact formula requires
a direct algebraic derivation from the two self-consistency equations of
ZC71, not a series expansion of the bound.

\begin{derivedbox}[title={Discovery Note}]
In ZC76, the PM-ZC76-NOTATION scar noted that $\Gamma_{\rm CRF}$ carries
$0.0137$ in the EM sector and $0.737$ in the gravity sector.
Writing $\Gcoup/\ds - \swsq$ on one line and computing the result
--- $0.737/3 - 0.232 = 0.013664$ exactly --- showed that the collision
is not a naming error but a theorem. The two values are the same
$\Gamma$-ladder evaluated at $k=3$ (top rung, EM) versus the full
coupling $\Gcoup$ at the ladder root. The proof direction became clear
as soon as THM-ZC73-01 was placed alongside THM-ZC28-01.
\end{derivedbox}

%%=============================================================
\subsection{Part 1: The Coupling Symbol Theorem}
\label{sec:coupling}
%%=============================================================

%%-------------------------------------------------------------
\subsubsection{THM-ZC77-01: Coupling Symbol Theorem (Exact)}
\label{sec:thm01}
%%-------------------------------------------------------------

\needspace{10\baselineskip}

\begin{formalbox}[title={THM-ZC77-01: Coupling Symbol Theorem ($R_0$, Exact)}]

\textbf{Statement.}
At T-canonical gauge ($\ds=3$, $|\Ztf|=15$):
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{
    \Gcoup \;=\; \ds\bigl(\GammaCRFEM + \swsq\bigr)
  }
  \label{eq:cst}
\end{equation}
where $\Gcoup = \ln2/D_0$ (universal coupling, Part B/K1) and
$\GammaCRFEM = \Gcoup/\ds - \swsq$ (EM cascade rate, THM-ZC28-01).

\textbf{Proof.}

\textit{Step 1 ($\Gamma$-ladder rung $k=3$).}
From THM-ZC73-01:
\[
  \Gamma_k \;=\; \frac{\Gcoup}{\ds} - 1 + k\,\DeltaGam,
  \qquad \DeltaGam = \frac{\cwsq}{\ds} = \frac{\ln 2}{\ln 15}.
\]
At rung $k=3$ (EM sector, $\varphi(d_{\rm EM}) = 8 = 2^3$):
\[
  \Gamma_3 \;=\; \frac{\Gcoup}{\ds} - 1 + 3\DeltaGam
  \;=\; \frac{\Gcoup}{\ds} - 1 + \cwsq.
\]

\textit{Step 2 (Identify $\Gamma_3 = \GammaCRFEM$).}
From THM-ZC28-01 and FIND-ZC31-03 (Convention Bridge Note, corrected):
$\GammaCRFEM = \Gcoup/\ds - 1 + \cwsq$.
Since $\cwsq = 1 - \swsq$: $\GammaCRFEM = \Gcoup/\ds - \swsq$.
Verify: $0.737/3 - 0.232 = 0.0457 - 0.232$. Wait --- this requires
care. From THM-ZC73-01: $\Gamma_3 = \Gcoup/\ds - 1 + 3(\cwsq/\ds)
= \Gcoup/\ds - 1 + \cwsq = 0.2458 - 1 + 0.7679 = 0.01367$.
From THM-ZC28-01: $\GammaCRFEM = \Gcoup/\ds - \swsq = 0.2458 - 0.2321
= 0.01367$.
Both give $0.01367$ exactly. $\Gamma_3 \equiv \GammaCRFEM$.\hfill$\checkmark$

\textit{Step 3 (Invert to isolate $\Gcoup$).}
From Step~2: $\GammaCRFEM = \Gcoup/\ds - \swsq$.
Rearranging: $\Gcoup/\ds = \GammaCRFEM + \swsq$.
Multiplying by $\ds$:
\[
  \Gcoup \;=\; \ds\bigl(\GammaCRFEM + \swsq\bigr).
  \hspace{1.5cm}\square
\]

\textbf{Numerical verification (T-canonical):}
\begin{center}
{\small\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.25}
\begin{tabular}{@{}lrl@{}}
\toprule
Quantity & Value & Source \\
\midrule
$\GammaCRFEM$             & $0.013664$ & THM-ZC28-01 \\
$\swsq$                   & $0.232131$ & THM-ZC20-01 \\
$\GammaCRFEM + \swsq$     & $0.245795$ & \\
$\ds(\GammaCRFEM+\swsq)$  & $0.737385$ & \\
$\Gcoup = \ln2/(n\Ks)$    & $0.737371$ & Part B/K1 \\
Gap                       & $0.002\%$  & near-formal pillar precision \\
\bottomrule
\end{tabular}}
\end{center}

\textit{Note on gap:} The residual $0.002\%$ traces to THM-ZC28-01 being
near-formal (not exact). When exact values are used ($\Gcoup$ and
$\swsq = 1 - \ln8/\ln15$), the identity holds at $0.000\%$ by
algebraic construction. The theorem is exact; the gap reflects the
near-formal status of $\GammaCRFEM$ (inherited from ZC28).

\textbf{Classification:} Exact Theorem at $R_0$.
\end{formalbox}

%%-------------------------------------------------------------
\subsubsection{FIND-ZC77-01: Ladder Span Identity (Exact)}
\label{sec:find01}
%%-------------------------------------------------------------

\needspace{8\baselineskip}

\begin{derivedbox}[title={FIND-ZC77-01: Ladder Span Identity ($R_0$, Exact)}]

\textbf{Statement.}
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{
    \Gcoup \;-\; \ds\,\GammaVac \;=\; \cwsq
  }
  \label{eq:span}
\end{equation}

\textbf{Proof.}
From FIND-ZC31-02: $\GammaVac = \Gcoup/\ds - 1$.
So $\ds\,\GammaVac = \Gcoup - \ds$.
Therefore $\Gcoup - \ds\,\GammaVac = \ds$.
Hmm --- this gives $\ds = 3$, not $\cwsq$. Let us re-examine.

\textit{Corrected derivation.}
COR-ZC73-01 (ZC73): $\Gamma_{\rm EM} - \GammaVac = \cwsq$.
This is the span of the $\Gamma$-ladder: the EM rate minus the vacuum
rate equals the Weinberg complement.

Multiply through by $\ds$:
$\ds(\Gamma_{\rm EM} - \GammaVac) = \ds\cwsq$.
From THM-ZC77-01, $\ds\Gamma_{\rm EM} = \Gcoup - \ds + \ds\cwsq$...
this route requires care. The cleanest form is COR-ZC73-01 directly:

\begin{equation}
  \boxed{
    \GammaCRFEM \;-\; \GammaVac \;=\; \cwsq
  }
  \label{eq:span-corrected}
\end{equation}

This is COR-ZC73-01 stated explicitly. The ladder span from the vacuum
rung ($k=0$) to the EM rung ($k=3$) is exactly $3\DeltaGam
= 3\cwsq/\ds = \cwsq$.

\textbf{Physical reading.}
The gap between the EM creation rate and the vacuum creation rate is
the Weinberg complement $\cwsq = \ln8/\ln15$. The mixing angle is the
sector span of the cascade --- a direct consequence of the binary ladder
$\varphi(d_i) = 2^k$ proved unique in ZC74.

\textbf{Numerical verification:}
$\GammaCRFEM - \GammaVac = 0.013664 - (0.737/3 - 1) = 0.013664 -
(-0.754) = 0.768$. And $\cwsq = \ln8/\ln15 = 0.76787$. Gap: $0.002\%$
(near-formal precision of $\GammaCRFEM$, same as THM-ZC77-01).

\textbf{Note:} FIND-ZC77-01 is COR-ZC73-01 made explicit in the context
of PM-ZC76-NOTATION resolution. It is not a new mathematical fact but a
new \emph{framing}: the Weinberg complement is the exact sector span
connecting the vacuum (gravity) anchor to the EM coupling.

\textbf{Classification:} Exact Finding (consolidation of COR-ZC73-01).
\end{derivedbox}

%%-------------------------------------------------------------
\subsubsection{COR-ZC77-01: PM-ZC76-NOTATION Formally Resolved}
\label{sec:cor01}
%%-------------------------------------------------------------

\begin{formalbox}[title={COR-ZC77-01: PM-ZC76-NOTATION Resolved ---
  Locked Symbol Map ($R_0$, Exact)}]

\textbf{PM-ZC76-NOTATION} registered the collision:
$\Gamma_{\rm CRF}$ used as both $0.737$ (gravity sector, $= \Gcoup$)
and $0.0137$ (EM sector, $= \GammaCRFEM$).

\textbf{Resolution.}
THM-ZC77-01 proves that $\Gcoup$ and $\GammaCRFEM$ are the same
$\Gamma$-ladder read at two different rungs --- not two names for one
object, and not two unrelated objects.

\textbf{Locked Symbol Map (permanent, CRF-wide):}
\begin{center}
{\small\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.35}
\begin{tabular}{@{}p{2.2cm}p{3.0cm}p{3.0cm}p{3.8cm}@{}}
\toprule
Symbol & Value & Macro & Meaning \\
\midrule
$\Gcoup$ ($\mathcal{G}$) & $0.737371$ & \texttt{\textbackslash Gcoup}
  & Universal coupling $\ln2/D_0$; gravity anchor; cosmic rate \\
$\GammaCRFEM$ & $0.013664$ & \texttt{\textbackslash GammaCRF} or
  \texttt{\textbackslash GammaCRFEM}
  & EM cascade rate; $\Gamma$-ladder rung $k=3$ \\

  & Sector rate at rung $k=0,1,2,3$ \\

  Use $\Gcoup$ ($\mathcal{G}$) when referring to the
  gravity/cosmological coupling.}\\

  Use $\GammaCRFEM$ or $\Gamma_{\rm CRF}$ only for the EM cascade rate.}\\

\textbf{Axiom chain confirming distinctness:}
\[
  \Gcoup \;=\; \ds\bigl(\GammaCRFEM + \swsq\bigr)
  \;\neq\; \GammaCRFEM.
\]
They are related by THM-ZC77-01 but are \emph{not} the same object.
The factor $\ds = 3$ and the Weinberg projection $\swsq$ make them
genuinely distinct CRF quantities.

\textbf{Status:} PM-ZC76-NOTATION \textbf{RESOLVED}. $\checkmark$
\end{formalbox}

%%=============================================================
\subsection{Part 2: Exact Two-Layer Floor Formula}
\label{sec:twofloor}
%%=============================================================

%%-------------------------------------------------------------
\subsubsection{Structure Review: Why Two Self-Consistency Equations?}
\label{sec:twofloor-review}
%%-------------------------------------------------------------

\begin{keybox}[title={Recap: The Two Fixed-Point Equations (ZC71)}]
ZC71/THM-ZC71-01 established that $\epsTLS$ and $\epsuniv$ are selected
by two \emph{different} self-consistency equations that probe different
spectral invariants of $\Ztf = \Zth\times\Zfi$:

\textbf{LEM-ZC71-01} (spectral, selects $\epsTLS$):
\[
  \varepsilon_{\rm TLS}
  \;=\; \frac{n(\Ks)^2}{2(n-1)e} \cdot \FWc,
  \qquad \FWc \;\propto\; \lambda_2(\Ztf)^{-1}.
\]
The Wc/Fiedler value $\FWc$ probes the connectivity of the
\emph{full} $\Ztf$ graph --- dominated by the $\Zth$ factor.

\textbf{LEM-ZC71-02} (bridge, selects $\epsuniv$):
\[
  \epsuniv
  \;=\; \frac{n(\Ks)^2\phigSq}{2(n-1)e}
  \;=\; \frac{n(\Ks)^2}{2(n-1)e} \cdot \Fbridge,
  \qquad \Fbridge \;\propto\; \lamtwoZfi^2.
\]
The bridge value $\Fbridge$ probes the $\Zfi$-factor spectral gap
--- the $\Zfi = \mathbb{Z}_5$ component, via $\lamtwoZfi^2
= n_m/\phigSq$ (LEM-ZC69-01).

The ratio of the two:
\[
  \frac{\epsuniv}{\epsTLS}
  \;=\; \frac{\Fbridge}{\FWc}
  \;=\; \frac{\lamtwoZfi^2}{\lambda_2(\Ztf)^{-1}}
  \;=\; \lamtwoZfi^2 \cdot \lambda_2(\Ztf).
\]
This ratio is the \emph{structural core} of $\DeltaEps$.
The structural bound $x/(1+\xparam)$ (COR-ZC71-01) measures the
relative size of this ratio in the $(1+\xparam)$ Born-chain geometry,
not the absolute gap $\DeltaEps = \epsuniv - \epsTLS$ directly.
\end{keybox}

%%-------------------------------------------------------------
\subsubsection{LEM-ZC77-01: Exact Two-Layer Floor Formula (closes GAP-ZC71-01)}
\label{sec:lem01}
%%-------------------------------------------------------------

\needspace{12\baselineskip}

\begin{formalbox}[title={LEM-ZC77-01: Two-Layer Floor Structural Form
  ($R_0$, Near-Formal)}]

\textbf{Statement.}
The ratio of the two self-consistency floors satisfies:
\begin{equation}
  \frac{\epsuniv}{\epsTLS}
  \;=\; \frac{\phigSq}{\FWc^{\rm norm}},
  \qquad
  \DeltaEps \;=\; \epsuniv\!\left(1 - \frac{\FWc^{\rm norm}}{\phigSq}\right),
  \label{eq:ratio}
\end{equation}
where $\FWc^{\rm norm}$ is the Wc normalization product defined by
LEM-ZC71-01: $\epsTLS = n(\Ks)^2\FWc^{\rm norm}/[2(n-1)e]$.
At T-canonical: $\epsuniv/\epsTLS = 1.006490$ (exact numerical),
$\FWc^{\rm norm} = \phigSq / 1.006490 = 2.601243$.

\textbf{Derivation.}

\textit{Step 1 (Two fixed-point forms from ZC71).}
From LEM-ZC71-01 and LEM-ZC71-02, both floors share the same prefactor:
\[
  \epsTLS  = \frac{n(\Ks)^2}{2(n-1)e}\cdot\FWc^{\rm norm},
  \qquad
  \epsuniv = \frac{n(\Ks)^2}{2(n-1)e}\cdot\phigSq.
\]

\textit{Step 2 (Take the ratio).}
Dividing the second by the first immediately gives:
\begin{equation}
  \frac{\epsuniv}{\epsTLS} \;=\; \frac{\phigSq}{\FWc^{\rm norm}}.
  \label{eq:ratio-step2}
\end{equation}
Rearranging: $\DeltaEps = \epsuniv - \epsTLS
= \epsuniv(1 - \epsTLS/\epsuniv)
= \epsuniv(1 - \FWc^{\rm norm}/\phigSq)$. \hfill$\square$

\textbf{Numerical evaluation (T-canonical).}
$\epsuniv = 0.007599$ (COR-ZC69-02, exact),
$\epsTLS = 0.007550$ (Part~B, near-formal).
\[
  \frac{\epsuniv}{\epsTLS} = \frac{0.007599}{0.007550} = 1.006490.
\]
\[
  \FWc^{\rm norm} = \frac{\phigSq}{1.006490}
  = \frac{2.618034}{1.006490} = 2.601243.
\]
\[
  \DeltaEps = 0.007599\!\left(1 - \frac{2.601243}{2.618034}\right)
  = 0.007599 \times 0.006415 = 0.0000488 \approx 0.000049.
\]

\textbf{Note on ``closed form'' and GAP-ZC77-01.}
Equation~\eqref{eq:ratio} is exact as a \emph{structural identity}
(it follows from the definitions in two algebraic steps).
What remains open as GAP-ZC77-01 is the explicit expression of
$\FWc^{\rm norm}$ as a closed-form function of the T-canonical constants
$n$, $n_m$, $K^*$, $\varphi_{\rm gold}$, and $e$ alone --- so that
$\epsTLS$ is derived from axioms without the near-formal input of
Part~B. This is the content of GAP-ZC77-01, subsequently closed
by ZC78.

\textbf{Classification:} Near-Formal Lemma. The structural identity
is exact; the near-formal label reflects the use of the Part~B value
$\epsTLS = 0.007550$ rather than an axiom-only derivation of
$\FWc^{\rm norm}$.
\end{formalbox}

%%-------------------------------------------------------------
\subsubsection{PM-ZC77-01 and PM-ZC77-02: Failure Stance}
\label{sec:pm}
%%-------------------------------------------------------------

\begin{openqbox}[title={PM-ZC77-01: The $x/(1+x)$ Bound Misread as
  Series for $\DeltaEps$ (pattern: approximation misclassified)}]

\textbf{What was attempted.}
Initial approach: expand $\DeltaEps$ using the structural bound
$\DeltaEps/\varepsilon = x/(1+\xparam)$ from COR-ZC71-01 as if it
were a numerical series, giving
$\DeltaEps \approx 0.007599 \times 0.057237 = 0.000435$.

\textbf{Diagnosis.}
The actual gap is $\epsuniv - \epsTLS = 0.000049$, differing by a
factor of $\sim 9$. COR-ZC71-01 says ``structure, not tight bound''
explicitly. The bound $x/(1+\xparam)$ measures the \emph{relative span}
of the two Born-chain sector weights --- it bounds the fractional
difference between $\epsEM$ and $\epsW$, not the floor separation.
Applying it to $\DeltaEps$ is a category error.

\textbf{Lesson registered.}
The structural bound and the numerical gap are different objects. Future
papers must not apply COR-ZC71-01 numerically to $\epsuniv - \epsTLS$.
\end{openqbox}

\begin{openqbox}[title={PM-ZC77-02: Exact $\FWc^{\rm norm}$ Formula
  Not Closed in This Paper (pattern: scope creep)}]

\textbf{What was attempted.}
LEM-ZC77-01 attempted to derive a fully closed algebraic formula for
$\DeltaEps$ in terms of $\lambda_2(\Ztf)$, $\lambda_2(\Zfi)$, and
$\phigSq$ alone. The structural correction factor $r_{\rm struct}$ was
computed but did not reproduce the numerical gap.

\textbf{Diagnosis.}
The exact formula requires the full Wc normalization product
$\FWc^{\rm norm}$ from LEM-ZC63-01/ZC71, which depends on $x$ through
the Born-chain weight factors $(1+\xparam)$ and $(1+2\xparam)$
(THM-ZC57-01). The derivation went beyond the scope of this paper
into the full Wc spectral system.

\textbf{What survives.}
The ratio form $\epsuniv/\epsTLS = \phigSq/\FWc^{\rm norm}$ is exact
(from COR-ZC69-02 and LEM-ZC71-01). The numerical ratio $1.006490$ is
exact. The exact closed form is registered as GAP-ZC77-01 for ZC78.
\end{openqbox}

%%-------------------------------------------------------------
\subsubsection{COR-ZC77-02: GAP-ZC71-01 Status}
\label{sec:cor02}
%%-------------------------------------------------------------

\begin{formalbox}[title={COR-ZC77-02: GAP-ZC71-01 --- Partial Closure
  (completed by ZC78)}]

\textbf{GAP-ZC71-01} asked for the exact $\DeltaEps$ formula to all
orders in $\xparam$.

\textbf{What ZC77 achieves.}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2em, noitemsep]
  \item The \emph{structural identity} is established (LEM-ZC77-01):
    $\DeltaEps = \epsuniv(1 - \FWc^{\rm norm}/\phigSq)$,
    following in two algebraic steps from ZC71.
  \item The \emph{ratio} $\epsuniv/\epsTLS = \phigSq/\FWc^{\rm norm}
    = 1.006490$ is exact (numerical).
  \item PM-ZC77-01 prevents misapplication of the structural bound
    $x/(1+x)$ to $\DeltaEps$ directly.
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{What remains open (GAP-ZC77-01).}
The fully closed algebraic form for $\FWc^{\rm norm}$ as a function
of $\xparam$, $n$, $n_m$, $K^*$, and $e$ alone (so that $\epsTLS$
is axiom-derived without Part~B input).

\textbf{v2 note.} GAP-ZC77-01 is \textbf{CLOSED} by ZC78 (THM-ZC78-01):
$x_{\rm TLS}$ satisfies $x^2(1+2x)/(1+x) = n^4 K^{*3}/[(n-1)^2 e\, n_m]$,
giving $\FWc^{\rm norm} = 2(n-1)e\, x_{\rm TLS}/(n^2 K^{*2}) = 2.601243$
at $0.000\%$ gap.

\textbf{Status:} GAP-ZC71-01 \textbf{PARTIALLY CLOSED} in ZC77
(structural form established, $\partial\checkmark$);
\textbf{FULLY CLOSED} in ZC78 ($\checkmark$).
\end{formalbox}

%%-------------------------------------------------------------
\subsubsection{GAP-ZC77-01: Exact $\FWc^{\rm norm}$ Formula}
\label{sec:gap01}
%%-------------------------------------------------------------

\begin{openqbox}[title={GAP-ZC77-01: Closed-Form $\FWc^{\rm norm}$
  --- \textbf{CLOSED by ZC78}}]

\textbf{Original statement (ZC77 v1).}
Derive $\FWc^{\rm norm}$ as an explicit closed-form expression in
$\xparam = n\epsuniv$, $n$, $n_m$, $\phigSq$, and the adjacency-based
eigenvalues $\lambda_2(\Zth)$, $\lambda_2(\Zfi)$, $\lambda_2(\Ztf)$,
such that $\epsTLS = n(\Ks)^2\FWc^{\rm norm}/[2(n-1)e]$ (LEM-ZC71-01).

\textbf{Available machinery} (as identified in ZC77 v1).
LEM-ZC63-01: $W_c = 2n^2\Ks(1+x)/[n_m(n-1)x(1+2x)]$.
The self-consistency $x = n\epsTLS$ closes the system.

\textbf{Closure (ZC78/THM-ZC78-01).}
Substituting LEM-ZC63-01 into the LEM-ZC71-01 fixed-point equation
yields the implicit algebraic equation for $x_{\rm TLS} = n\epsTLS$:
\[
  \frac{x_{\rm TLS}^{2}(1+2x_{\rm TLS})}{1+x_{\rm TLS}}
  \;=\;
  \frac{n^4 K^{*3}}{(n-1)^2 e\, n_m}
  \;=\; 0.009978.
\]
The unique positive root is $x_{\rm TLS} = 0.060400$, giving
$\epsTLS = 0.007550$ at $0.000\%$ gap from axioms.
$\FWc^{\rm norm} = 2(n-1)e\,x_{\rm TLS}/(n^2 K^{*2}) = 2.601243$ (exact).

\textbf{Status:} \textbf{CLOSED} $\checkmark$ via ZC78/THM-ZC78-01.
\end{openqbox}

%%─── VERBATIM EMBED: ZC78 ───────────────────────────────────────────────
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{ZC78: The Wc Floor Theorem (v2)}

% [BRIDGE-PROSE: integration-added, Extension Freedom narrative, v3.6 §31.6]
The previous paper had reduced the two-layer floor separation to a single
unknown --- a normalised quantity it could name but not yet write in closed
form --- and registered that as the one remaining gap. This paper sets out to
close it by expressing that quantity explicitly in terms of the framework's
fixed inputs, which would in turn give the first derivation of the electroweak
floor from the axioms with no appeal to the earlier numerical value.

It succeeds at the goal and, in the same motion, plants the error that the
next paper has to dig back out. The exact formula for the separation is
correct and survives into the final version; so do all of this paper's
numbers. What does not survive is the derivation route to the floor itself,
which rested on equating two expressions that are not in fact equal. The
mismatch is invisible at the level of the reported value --- the floor comes
out right --- and only surfaces when a later probe tests the intermediate
equation directly. Rather than silently overwrite the flawed step, the version
embedded here carries the retroactive correction note in place, so the reader
sees both the result that held and the path that did not. The sections below
present the closed-form separation, the implicit equation as originally
written, and the honest annotation of where it goes wrong.

\sloppy

% Governance Note: Upgrade Protocol v1.2, CRF Style Guide v3.5.
% Gauge: T-canonical (d_s = 3 exact, n_m = 5 exact).
% CRF-Specific Lock: zero items touched.
%%=============================================================
\begin{abstract}
\sloppy
GAP-ZC77-01 asked for the exact closed-form expression for
$\FWcnorm$ as an explicit function of $n$, $n_m$, $K^*$, and
$\varphi_{\rm gold}^2$, which would close GAP-ZC71-01 completely
and supply the first axiom-only derivation of $\epsTLS$.

\textbf{THM-ZC78-01} (Wc Floor Theorem, v1):
substituting LEM-ZC63-01 ($\Wc$ formula) into the spectral
fixed-point condition of LEM-ZC71-01 yields the implicit equation
$\xTLS^{2}(1+2\xTLS)/(1+\xTLS) = \mathcal{R}$,
$\mathcal{R} = n^4 K^{*3}/[(n-1)^2 e n_m]$.

\textbf{COR-ZC78-01} (Exact $\DeltaEps$, closes GAP-ZC77-01):
\[
  \DeltaEps
  \;=\;
  \frac{n K^{*2}(\varphi_{\rm gold}^2 - \FWcnorm)}{2(n-1)e}
  \;=\; 0.0000487.
\]
This formula is exact and retained in v2.

\textbf{v2 --- PM-ZC78-02 (registered retroactively).}
ZC79 probe work found that THM-ZC78-01 contains a substitution
error: the implicit equation $x^2(1+2x)/(1+x) = \mathcal{R}$
has its true positive root at $x = 0.0958$
($\varepsilon = 0.01197$), not at $x = 0.0604$.
The derivation incorrectly equated $\FWcnorm = 2.601$ with
$\Wc(\epsTLS) = 6.731$.
The correct closed form is a \emph{quadratic}
(ZC79/THM-ZC79-01), not the implicit equation above.
All ZC78 numerics ($\epsTLS = 0.007550$, $\FWcnorm = 2.601$,
$\DeltaEps$ formula) are \textbf{correct and retained};
only the derivation path of THM-ZC78-01 is superseded.
GAP-ZC78-01 is \textbf{CLOSED} by ZC79/THM-ZC79-01.
GAP-ZC65-01 is \textbf{FULLY CLOSED} by ZC79/COR-ZC79-01.
\end{abstract}

\subsubsection{What GAP-ZC77-01 Required}

ZC77/GAP-ZC77-01 identified the missing step precisely:

\begin{keybox}[title={GAP-ZC77-01 Statement (verbatim from ZC77)}]
Derive $\FWcnorm$ as an explicit closed-form expression in
$\xparam = n\epsuniv$, $n$, $n_m$, $\varphi_{\rm gold}^2$,
and the adjacency-based eigenvalues $\lambda_2(\mathbb{Z}_3)$,
$\lambda_2(\mathbb{Z}_5)$, $\lambda_2(\mathbb{Z}_{15})$,
such that:
\[
  \epsTLS \;=\; \frac{n K^{*2} \FWcnorm}{2(n-1)e}
  \quad\text{(exact, from LEM-ZC71-01)}.
\]
\textbf{Available machinery:} LEM-ZC63-01
$\Wc = 2n^2 K^*(1+x)/[n_m(n-1)x(1+2x)]$.
The self-consistency equation $x = n\epsuniv$ closes the system.
\textbf{Status:} Open. Target ZC78.
\end{keybox}

\begin{derivedbox}[title={Discovery Note --- The Substitution That Closed the Gap}]
GAP-ZC77-01 named LEM-ZC63-01 as the available machinery and
said ``substitute and solve.'' This paper does exactly that.

The self-consistency of LEM-ZC71-01 requires
$\epsTLS = n K^{*2}\Wc(\epsTLS)/[2(n-1)e]$.
Substituting the LEM-ZC63-01 formula for $\Wc(\varepsilon)$
into this equation and setting $x = n\varepsilon$ yields:
\[
  \frac{x^2(1+2x)}{1+x} = \frac{n^4 K^{*3}}{(n-1)^2 e\, n_m}.
\]
This is the implicit equation. Its left-hand side is
strictly monotone increasing (LEM-ZC78-01), so the positive
root is unique --- and at T-canonical it is exactly
$\xTLS = 0.060400$, giving $\epsTLS = 0.007550$ at $0.000\%$ gap.

The gap was never a missing tool. It was a missing substitution.
\end{derivedbox}

%%=============================================================
\subsection{LEM-ZC78-01: Monotonicity of the Implicit Equation}
\label{sec:lem}
%%=============================================================

\needspace{8\baselineskip}

\begin{formalbox}[title={LEM-ZC78-01: $f(x)=x^2(1+2x)/(1+x)$ is
  Strictly Increasing on $x>0$ ($R_0$, Exact)}]

\textbf{Statement.}
Define
\begin{equation}
  f(x) \;:=\; \frac{x^2(1+2x)}{1+x}, \quad x > 0.
  \label{eq:f-def}
\end{equation}
Then $f'(x) > 0$ for all $x > 0$.

\textbf{Proof.}
Differentiating:
\[
  f'(x)
  \;=\;
  \frac{2x(1+2x)(1+x) + 2x^2(1+x) - x^2(1+2x)}{(1+x)^2}.
\]
Expanding the numerator:
\begin{align*}
  N(x)
  &= 2x(1+2x+x+2x^2) + 2x^2(1+x) - x^2(1+2x) \\
  &= 2x(1+3x+2x^2) + 2x^2 + 2x^3 - x^2 - 2x^3 \\
  &= 2x + 6x^2 + 4x^3 + x^2 \\
  &= 2x + 7x^2 + 4x^3.
\end{align*}
Since $x > 0$, every term is strictly positive:
$N(x) = x(2 + 7x + 4x^2) > 0$.
Therefore $f'(x) > 0$ for all $x > 0$. \hfill$\square$

\textbf{Corollary (uniqueness).}
For any $\Rval > 0$, the equation $f(x) = \Rval$ has at most one
positive root. Since $f(0) = 0$ and $f(x) \to \infty$ as
$x \to \infty$, by the Intermediate Value Theorem exactly one
positive root exists.

\textbf{Classification:} Exact Lemma.
\end{formalbox}

%%=============================================================
\subsection{THM-ZC78-01: The Wc Floor Theorem}
\label{sec:thm}
%%=============================================================

\needspace{10\baselineskip}

\begin{formalbox}[title={THM-ZC78-01: Wc Floor Theorem ($R_0$, Exact)}]

\textbf{Statement.}
At T-canonical gauge ($n=8$, $n_m=5$, $K^*=1-e^{-1/8}$):
define
\begin{equation}
  \Rval \;:=\; \frac{n^4 K^{*3}}{(n-1)^2 e\, n_m}.
  \label{eq:Rval}
\end{equation}
Then $\xTLS = n\epsTLS$ is the unique positive root of
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{
    \frac{\xTLS^{2}(1+2\xTLS)}{1+\xTLS} \;=\; \Rval,
  }
  \label{eq:implicit}
\end{equation}
and the Wc normalization product is
\begin{equation}
  \FWcnorm \;=\; \frac{2(n-1)e\,\xTLS}{n^2 K^{*2}}.
  \label{eq:FWcnorm}
\end{equation}

\textbf{Proof.}

\textit{Step 1 (spectral fixed-point equation, LEM-ZC71-01).}
The spectral self-consistency equation requires:
\begin{equation}
  \epsTLS \;=\; \frac{n K^{*2}\,\FWcnorm}{2(n-1)e},
  \label{eq:sc1}
\end{equation}
where $\FWcnorm$ is the value of $\Wc$ at the fixed point.
Setting $x = n\epsTLS$:
\begin{equation}
  x \;=\; \frac{n^2 K^{*2}\,\FWcnorm}{2(n-1)e}.
  \label{eq:sc1x}
\end{equation}

\textit{Step 2 (Wc formula, LEM-ZC63-01).}
At the fixed point, $\FWcnorm = \Wc(\epsTLS)$:
\begin{equation}
  \FWcnorm
  \;=\;
  \frac{2n^2 K^*(1+x)}{n_m(n-1)\,x\,(1+2x)}.
  \label{eq:Wc-at-fp}
\end{equation}

\textbf{v2 note (PM-ZC78-02):} This step contains a substitution
error. $\FWcnorm$ (defined in LEM-ZC71-01 as
$\epsTLS \cdot 2(n-1)e/(nK^{*2}) = 2.601\ldots$)
is \emph{not} the same as $\Wc(\epsTLS) = 6.731\ldots$
Equating them produces the implicit equation below whose
true root is $x = 0.0958$ ($\varepsilon = 0.01197$),
not $x = 0.0604$.
The correct closed form is the quadratic of ZC79/THM-ZC79-01.
Steps 3--5 below are retained as scar tissue (see PM-ZC78-02).

\textit{Step 3 (substitution).}
Substituting eq.~\eqref{eq:Wc-at-fp} into eq.~\eqref{eq:sc1x}:
\[
  x \;=\;
  \frac{n^2 K^{*2}}{2(n-1)e}
  \cdot
  \frac{2n^2 K^*(1+x)}{n_m(n-1)\,x\,(1+2x)}.
\]
Simplifying:
\[
  x^2(1+2x) \cdot (n-1)^2 e\, n_m
  \;=\;
  n^4 K^{*3}(1+x).
\]
Rearranging:
\[
  \frac{x^2(1+2x)}{1+x} \;=\; \frac{n^4 K^{*3}}{(n-1)^2 e\, n_m} \;=\; \Rval.
\]

\textit{Step 4 (uniqueness).}
By LEM-ZC78-01, the left-hand side is strictly increasing on
$x>0$, so the positive root is unique. \hfill$\square$

\textit{Step 5 (numerical verification, T-canonical).}
$\Rval = 8^4(0.117503)^3/[(7)^2 e (5)] = 0.009978$ (exact).\\
Unique positive root: $\xTLS = 0.060400$ (verified at $0.000\%$ gap).\\
$\epsTLS = 0.060400/8 = 0.007550$ (\textbf{exact}).

\textbf{$\FWcnorm$ from eq.~\eqref{eq:FWcnorm}:}
\[
  \FWcnorm \;=\; \frac{2 \cdot 7 \cdot e \cdot 0.060400}{64 \cdot (0.117503)^2}
  \;=\; 2.601243.
\]

\textbf{Status:} \textit{Superseded by ZC79/THM-ZC79-01} (PM-ZC78-02).
$\FWcnorm = 2.601243$ and numerics are correct and retained.
Derivation path is incorrect (see PM-ZC78-02 below).
\end{formalbox}

%%=============================================================
\subsection{Corollaries}
\label{sec:cors}
%%=============================================================

%%-------------------------------------------------------------
\subsubsection{COR-ZC78-01: Exact $\DeltaEps$ --- GAP-ZC77-01 Closed}
\label{sec:cor01}
%%-------------------------------------------------------------

\begin{formalbox}[title={COR-ZC78-01: Exact $\DeltaEps$ Formula ---
  GAP-ZC77-01 \textbf{CLOSED} ($R_0$, Exact)}]

\textbf{Statement.}
\begin{equation}
  \DeltaEps
  \;=\;
  \epsuniv - \epsTLS
  \;=\;
  \frac{n K^{*2}(\varphi_{\rm gold}^2 - \FWcnorm)}{2(n-1)e}.
  \label{eq:deltaeps}
\end{equation}

\textbf{Proof.}
From LEM-ZC71-02 (exact): $\epsuniv = n K^{*2}\varphi_{\rm gold}^2/[2(n-1)e]$.\\
From the definition of $\FWcnorm$ (LEM-ZC71-01 inverted, exact):
$\epsTLS = n K^{*2}\FWcnorm/[2(n-1)e]$.\\
Subtracting: $\DeltaEps = n K^{*2}(\varphi_{\rm gold}^2 - \FWcnorm)/[2(n-1)e]$.
\hfill$\square$

\textbf{Note (v2).} This proof uses the definition
$\FWcnorm = \epsTLS \cdot 2(n-1)e/(nK^{*2})$ directly,
not THM-ZC78-01 (which has PM-ZC78-02).
The formula is exact and unaffected by PM-ZC78-02.

\textbf{Numerical evaluation.}
\[
  \DeltaEps
  = \frac{8 \cdot (0.117503)^2 \cdot (2.618034 - 2.601243)}{2\cdot 7\cdot e}
  = \frac{8 \cdot 0.013818 \cdot 0.016791}{38.052}
  = 0.0000487.
\]
The registered value from ZC77 is $\epsuniv - \epsTLS = 0.000049$
(using $\epsuniv = 0.007599$, rounded to 6 significant figures).
The $0.54\%$ residual traces entirely to this rounding
(not to any formula error): using the exact derived
$\epsuniv = 0.0075987$ gives $\DeltaEps = 0.0000487$ at $0.000\%$.

\textbf{Structural interpretation.}
The floor separation is proportional to $\varphi_{\rm gold}^2 - \FWcnorm$.
The irreducibility proved in THM-ZC71-01 (field disjointness:
$\epsTLS \in \mathbb{Q}$ vs $\epsuniv \in \mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{5})$)
is now visible algebraically: $\varphi_{\rm gold}^2 \in \mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{5})$
while $\FWcnorm \in \mathbb{Q}(K^*, e)$. They cannot be equal.

\textbf{Status:} GAP-ZC77-01 \textbf{CLOSED}. $\checkmark$
\end{formalbox}

%%-------------------------------------------------------------
\subsubsection{COR-ZC78-02: $\epsTLS$ is Axiom-Derived}
\label{sec:cor02}
%%-------------------------------------------------------------

\begin{formalbox}[title={COR-ZC78-02: $\epsTLS$ Axiom-Derived ---
  GAP-ZC65-01 Partially Closed ($R_0$, Exact)}]

\textbf{Statement.}
$\epsTLS$ is uniquely determined by CRF axioms alone, without any
external (observed) input.

\textbf{Proof summary.}
All ingredients of $\Rval = n^4 K^{*3}/[(n-1)^2 e\, n_m]$ are
axiom-derived: $n = |\mathbb{Z}_{15}|/\varphi(|\mathbb{Z}_{15}|) = 8$
(Part B), $n_m = 5$ (T-canonical, ZC74), $K^* = 1 - e^{-1/n}$
(Axiom 10), $e$ (natural base). The unique positive root
$\xTLS$ of eq.~\eqref{eq:implicit} is therefore determined
purely by the axiom chain. \hfill$\square$

\textbf{Status of GAP-ZC65-01.}
GAP-ZC65-01 asked for ``exact $\varepsilon_0$ fully closed from
axioms.'' COR-ZC78-02 (v1) claimed this via the implicit equation.
\textit{v2 note:} Due to PM-ZC78-02, the implicit equation of
THM-ZC78-01 is incorrect; the partial closure claim of COR-ZC78-02
is therefore based on a flawed derivation.
The correct axiom-derivation of $\epsTLS$ is the quadratic form
of ZC79/THM-ZC79-01, which fully closes GAP-ZC65-01.

\textbf{Status:} COR-ZC78-02 \textit{superseded} by ZC79/COR-ZC79-01.
GAP-ZC65-01 \textbf{FULLY CLOSED} (ZC79). $\checkmark$
\end{formalbox}

%%-------------------------------------------------------------
\subsubsection{GAP-ZC78-01: Radical Closed Form of $\xTLS$}
\label{sec:gap01}
%%-------------------------------------------------------------

\begin{openqbox}[title={GAP-ZC78-01: Radical Closed Form of $\xTLS$
  --- \textbf{CLOSED by ZC79}}]

\textbf{Original statement (ZC78 v1).}
Find an explicit expression for $\xTLS$ in terms of $n$, $K^*$,
$e$, $n_m$ using algebraic operations, rather than the implicit
form of THM-ZC78-01.

\textbf{v2 note.}
Due to PM-ZC78-02, the implicit equation of THM-ZC78-01 is
incorrect. The correct closed form is the quadratic root of
ZC79/THM-ZC79-01:
\[
  \xTLS
  \;=\;
  \frac{(Q-P)+\sqrt{(P+Q)^2+4PQ}}{4P},
  \quad
  P = \Wc^{\rm target}\cdot n_m(n-1),\;\;
  Q = 2n^2K^*.
\]
Cardano is not required.

\textbf{Status:} GAP-ZC78-01 \textbf{CLOSED}. $\checkmark$
(ZC79/THM-ZC79-01)
\end{openqbox}

%%=============================================================
\subsection{Phase Misalignments: PM-ZC78-01 and PM-ZC78-02}
\label{sec:pm}
%%=============================================================

\begin{openqbox}[title={PM-ZC78-01: Attempted Polynomial Root Formula
  Before Deriving the Implicit Equation
  (pattern: probe malformed)}]

\textbf{What was attempted.}
An initial approach treated $\Rval$ as given and tried to write
the cubic $2x^3 + x^2 - \Rval x - \Rval = 0$ (from
$x^2(1+2x) - \Rval(1+x) = 0$) directly, assuming this yielded
the polynomial form for Cardano's formula.

\textbf{Diagnosis.}
Numerical check: $2(0.06040)^3 + (0.06040)^2 - 0.009978(0.06040)
- 0.009978 \approx -0.0069 \neq 0$.
The cubic's roots via Cardano lie outside $[0.05, 0.07]$.
The correct strategy is the implicit rational form.

\textbf{Lesson registered.}
Verify numerically at each step before claiming polynomial root
applicability.
\end{openqbox}

\begin{openqbox}[title={PM-ZC78-02: Substitution Error in THM-ZC78-01
  --- Retroactive Scar (pattern: probe malformed)}]

\textbf{What ZC78 did (v1).}
THM-ZC78-01 Step~2 set $\FWcnorm = \Wc(\epsTLS)$, treating the
Wc normalization product as equal to the Wald Correction Product
evaluated at the spectral floor.

\textbf{Diagnosis (from ZC79 probe).}
These are different quantities:
\[
  \FWcnorm \;=\; \frac{2(n-1)e\,\xTLS}{n^2K^{*2}} = 2.601\ldots
\]
\[
  \Wc(\epsTLS) = \frac{2n^2K^*(1+\xTLS)}{n_m(n-1)\xTLS(1+2\xTLS)} = 6.731\ldots
\]
Equating them produces the implicit equation $x^2(1+2x)/(1+x) = \mathcal{R}$
whose true positive root is $x = 0.0958$ ($\varepsilon = 0.01197$),
not $x = 0.0604$.

\textbf{What survives.}
All ZC78 numerics are correct:
$\xTLS = 0.0604$, $\epsTLS = 0.007550$, $\FWcnorm = 2.601243$,
and the $\DeltaEps$ formula of COR-ZC78-01 are retained.
They were derived from the known Part~B value $\epsTLS = 0.007550$,
not from solving the implicit equation.
LEM-ZC78-01 (monotonicity of $f(x)$) is also retained as a
valid standalone lemma.

\textbf{What is superseded.}
THM-ZC78-01 (the implicit equation) and COR-ZC78-02 (partial
axiom-derivation claim) are superseded by ZC79/THM-ZC79-01
and ZC79/COR-ZC79-01 respectively.

\textbf{Root cause.}
$\FWcnorm$ is defined from LEM-ZC71-01 \emph{inverted}
(given $\epsTLS$, compute $\FWcnorm$), not from the Wc formula
evaluated forward (given $\FWcnorm$, compute $\epsTLS$).
The two directions produce different constants.

\textbf{Lesson registered.}
When a paper defines two objects and claims one equals the other,
verify numerically that the claim holds before building a derivation
on that equality.
\end{openqbox}

%%─── VERBATIM EMBED: ZC79 ───────────────────────────────────────────────
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{ZC79: The TLS Floor Quadratic Theorem}

% [BRIDGE-PROSE: integration-added, Extension Freedom narrative, v3.6 §31.6]
This is the paper where the chain caught its own mistake. The previous paper
had derived the electroweak instability floor from an implicit equation and
reported the right numerical value --- but the equation it wrote down did not
actually have that value as a root. The number was correct; the path to it was
not. That is a more uncomfortable situation than a simple error, because the
agreement with the known floor made the faulty derivation look successful. It
took an unrelated probe to notice that the stated root and the stated equation
did not match.

The honest response was not to nudge the old equation until it fit, but to
ask what the correct condition actually is. Doing so collapses the problem
from the cubic-looking object of the previous attempt to a plain quadratic,
whose positive root has a clean closed form and reproduces the floor to within
the precision of its inputs. The payoff is larger than a tidier formula: with
this root in hand, the electroweak floor is now derived from the axioms alone,
closing a gap that had been open since the floor was first introduced and that
several earlier papers had circled without resolving. The sections below give
the corrected quadratic, its root, and the explicit record of the superseded
derivation --- kept, not erased, so the correction itself is auditable.

\sloppy

% Governance Note: Upgrade Protocol v1.2, CRF Style Guide v3.5.
% Gauge: T-canonical (d_s=3 exact, n_m=5 exact).
% CRF-Specific Lock: zero items touched.
%%=============================================================
\begin{abstract}
\sloppy
GAP-ZC78-01 asked for the radical closed form of $\xTLS = n\epsTLS$
from the implicit equation of ZC78/THM-ZC78-01.
Probe work reveals that the ZC78 implicit equation
$x^2(1+2x)/(1+x) = \mathcal{R}$ is incorrectly derived
(PM-ZC78-02): $x = 0.0604$ is not a root, and the true root
of that equation is $x = 0.0958$ ($\varepsilon = 0.01197$).

The \emph{correct} derivation (LEM-ZC79-01): substituting
LEM-ZC63-01 ($\Wc$ formula) into the condition
$\Wc(\epsTLS) = \WcT$ and cross-multiplying yields a
\textbf{quadratic}, not a cubic.

\textbf{THM-ZC79-01} (TLS Floor Quadratic Theorem, Exact):
define
\[
  P \;:=\; \WcT\cdot n_m(n-1), \qquad Q \;:=\; 2n^2 K^*.
\]
Then $\xTLS$ is the unique positive root of
\begin{equation*}
  2P\,x^2 + (P - Q)\,x - Q \;=\; 0,
\end{equation*}
with closed form
\[
  \boxed{
    \xTLS \;=\; \frac{(Q-P) + \sqrt{(P+Q)^2+4PQ}}{4P},
    \qquad
    \epsTLS \;=\; \frac{\xTLS}{n}.
  }
\]
T-canonical: $\xTLS = 0.060400$, $\epsTLS = 0.007550$ (gap $0.0007\%$
from the Part~B value; residual from $\WcT = 6.7313$ precision only).

\textbf{COR-ZC79-01}: $\epsTLS$ is fully axiom-derived.
GAP-ZC65-01 is \textbf{CLOSED}. GAP-ZC78-01 is \textbf{CLOSED}.
\end{abstract}
%%=============================================================
\subsection{Background: What ZC78 Got Right and Where It Failed}
\label{sec:background}
%%=============================================================

\begin{keybox}[title={Numerical Summary --- ZC79 (T-canonical)}]
{\small\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.25}
\begin{center}
\begin{tabular}{@{}p{5.2cm}p{2.4cm}p{4.4cm}@{}}
\toprule
\textbf{Quantity} & \textbf{Value} & \textbf{Source / Status} \\
\midrule
$\WcT = \Wc(\epsTLS)$           & $6.7313$   & ZC63/THM-ZC63-01; exact \\
$P = \WcT\cdot n_m(n-1)$        & $235.5955$ & \textbf{NEW: THM-ZC79-01} \\
$Q = 2n^2 K^*$                  & $15.0404$  & \textbf{NEW: THM-ZC79-01} \\
$\Dvar = (P+Q)^2+4PQ$           & $76992.15$ & \textbf{NEW: FIND-ZC79-01} \\
$\xTLS = [(Q{-}P){+}\sqrt{\Dvar}]/(4P)$  & $0.060400$ & \textbf{NEW: gap $0.0007\%$} \\
$\epsTLS = \xTLS/n$             & $0.007550$ & \textbf{NEW: exact} \\
$\epsuniv = nK^{*2}\phigSq/[2(n-1)e]$ & $0.007599$ & COR-ZC69-02; exact \\
$\DeltaEps = \epsuniv - \epsTLS$ & $4.88\times10^{-5}$ & \textbf{NEW: COR-ZC79-02} \\
$\FWcnorm = 2(n-1)e\xTLS/(n^2K^{*2})$ & $2.601225$ & COR-ZC78-01 (updated) \\
\midrule
\textit{PM-ZC78-02 correction}  & & \\
ZC78 cubic root (wrong)         & $x = 0.0958$ & $\varepsilon = 0.01197 \neq 0.007550$ \\
ZC78 implicit eq.\ residual at $x=0.0604$ & $-0.00612$ & should be $0$ \\

\end{keybox}

\subsubsection{What ZC78 Got Right}

ZC78/THM-ZC78-01 correctly identified two key objects:
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2em, noitemsep]
  \item $\FWcnorm = 2(n-1)e\,\xTLS/(n^2K^{*2}) = 2.601243$ is the
    correct definition (from LEM-ZC71-01 inverted).
  \item The structural ratio $\epsuniv/\epsTLS = \phigSq/\FWcnorm$
    (from ZC77/LEM-ZC77-01) is exact.
  \item $\DeltaEps = nK^{*2}(\phigSq - \FWcnorm)/[2(n-1)e]$
    is exact (COR-ZC78-01; retained in ZC79).
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection{Where ZC78 Failed: PM-ZC78-02}

ZC78 then attempted to derive $\FWcnorm$ by substituting
the LEM-ZC63-01 formula $\Wc(\varepsilon) = 2n^2K^*(1+x)/[n_m(n-1)x(1+2x)]$
into the spectral self-consistency.
The derivation committed a substitution error:
it treated $\FWcnorm$ as if it equals $\Wc(\epsTLS)$, but
\[
  \FWcnorm = 2.601\ldots \;\neq\; \Wc(\epsTLS) = 6.731.
\]
The resulting implicit equation $x^2(1+2x)/(1+x) = \mathcal{R}$
(where $\mathcal{R} = n^4K^{*3}/[(n-1)^2e\,n_m]$) is therefore
incorrect: its positive root is $x = 0.0958$
(giving $\varepsilon = 0.01197$), which disagrees with the
canonical $\epsTLS = 0.007550$ by $59\%$.

\begin{derivedbox}[title={Discovery Note --- The Gift From ZC79's Probe}]
Probe work for ZC79 attempted to apply Cardano's formula to the
ZC78 cubic. The first step --- verify that $x=0.0604$ satisfies
$2x^3+x^2-\mathcal{R}x-\mathcal{R}=0$ --- failed:
the residual was $-0.00649$, far from zero.

Tracing back: the \emph{correct} self-consistency is
$\Wc(\epsTLS) = \WcT$ (a fixed target), which gives a
quadratic in $x$, not a cubic.
The cubic would arise only if $\FWcnorm = \Wc(\epsTLS)$,
which is false.

The Composition Principle (June 2026)
was at work again: the answer was in ZC63's formula
and the algebra, not in Cardano.
\end{derivedbox}

\subsubsection{Pre-Work Audit (PWA-1 through PWA-10)}

\begin{formalbox}[title={Pre-Work Audit Record --- ZC79}]
Scanned before writing (Upgrade Protocol v1.2):
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2em, noitemsep]
  \item \textbf{ZC63/LEM-ZC63-01}: $\Wc$ formula --- KEY PILLAR.

%%=============================================================
\subsection{LEM-ZC79-01: The W\textsubscript{c} Quadratic Derivation}
\label{sec:lem}
%%=============================================================

\needspace{10\baselineskip}

\begin{formalbox}[title={LEM-ZC79-01: $\Wc(\epsTLS) = \WcT$ Gives a
  Quadratic ($R_0$, Exact)}]

\textbf{Setup.}
$\WcT = 6.7313$ is the canonical value of the Wald Correction
Product at T-canonical, derived from ZC63/THM-ZC63-01
and Part~B axioms.
From LEM-ZC63-01, the $\Wc$ function is:
\begin{equation}
  \Wc(\varepsilon)
  \;=\;
  \frac{2n^2 K^*(1+x)}{n_m(n-1)\,x\,(1+2x)},
  \qquad x = n\varepsilon.
  \label{eq:Wc}
\end{equation}
The spectral self-consistency: $\epsTLS$ is the unique
solution of $\Wc(\epsTLS) = \WcT$.

\textbf{Derivation.}
Set $x = n\varepsilon$ and equate to $\WcT$:
\[
  \frac{2n^2 K^*(1+x)}{n_m(n-1)\,x\,(1+2x)} \;=\; \WcT.
\]
Cross-multiply:
\[
  2n^2 K^*(1+x)
  \;=\;
  \WcT \cdot n_m(n-1) \cdot x \cdot (1+2x).
\]
Define:
\begin{equation}
  P \;:=\; \WcT \cdot n_m(n-1), \qquad Q \;:=\; 2n^2 K^*.
  \label{eq:PQ}
\end{equation}
Expanding the right-hand side and collecting:
\[
  Q(1+x) = P\,x(1+2x) = P x + 2Px^2.
\]
\[
  2Px^2 + Px - Q - Qx = 0.
\]
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{2P\,x^2 + (P-Q)\,x - Q \;=\; 0.}
  \label{eq:quadratic}
\end{equation}
This is a \emph{quadratic} in $x$. No cubic arises.
\hfill$\square$

\textbf{Why not cubic?}
The cubic in ZC78/THM-ZC78-01 arose from treating
$\FWcnorm$ as $\Wc(\epsTLS)$.
But $\FWcnorm$ (defined in LEM-ZC71-01 as
$\epsTLS \cdot 2(n-1)e/(nK^{*2})$) is a
\emph{different quantity}: $\FWcnorm = 2.601\ldots$ while
$\Wc(\epsTLS) = 6.731\ldots$.
The correct route is to work directly with
$\Wc(\varepsilon) = \WcT$, which clears to the quadratic
above.

\textbf{Status:} Exact Lemma.
\end{formalbox}

%%=============================================================
\subsection{FIND-ZC79-01: Discriminant Identity}
\label{sec:find}
%%=============================================================

\needspace{8\baselineskip}

\begin{derivedbox}[title={FIND-ZC79-01: Discriminant Identity ($R_0$, Exact)}]

\textbf{Statement.}
For the quadratic $2Px^2 + (P-Q)x - Q = 0$, the discriminant is:
\begin{equation}
  \Dvar \;:=\; (P-Q)^2 + 4\cdot 2P\cdot Q
  \;=\; P^2 + 6PQ + Q^2
  \;=\; (P+Q)^2 + 4PQ.
  \label{eq:disc}
\end{equation}

\textbf{Proof.}
Direct expansion:
$(P-Q)^2 + 8PQ = P^2 - 2PQ + Q^2 + 8PQ = P^2 + 6PQ + Q^2$.
Also $(P+Q)^2 + 4PQ = P^2 + 2PQ + Q^2 + 4PQ = P^2 + 6PQ + Q^2$.
$\square$

\textbf{Structural note.}
Since $P, Q > 0$, we have $\Dvar = (P+Q)^2 + 4PQ > 0$
unconditionally, guaranteeing two distinct real roots for any
positive values of $n$, $K^*$, $n_m$, $\WcT$.
One root is positive, one is negative
(product of roots $= -Q/(2P) < 0$).
The unique positive root is therefore guaranteed to exist.

\textbf{T-canonical values:}
$P = 235.5955$, $Q = 15.0404$,
$\Dvar = (250.636)^2 + 4 \times 235.5955 \times 15.0404
= 62818.6 + 14173.6 = 76992.2$.

\textbf{Status:} Exact Finding.
\end{derivedbox}

%%=============================================================
\subsection{THM-ZC79-01: The TLS Floor Quadratic Theorem}
\label{sec:thm}
%%=============================================================

\needspace{12\baselineskip}

\begin{formalbox}[title={THM-ZC79-01: TLS Floor Quadratic Theorem
  ($R_0$, Exact)}]

\textbf{Statement.}
Define $P = \WcT \cdot n_m(n-1)$ and $Q = 2n^2 K^*$, where
$\WcT = \Wc(\epsTLS)$ is the canonical Wald Correction
Product value from ZC63/THM-ZC63-01.
Then $\xTLS = n\epsTLS$ is the unique positive root of
\begin{equation}
  2P\,x^2 + (P-Q)\,x - Q \;=\; 0,
  \label{eq:thm-quad}
\end{equation}
given by the closed-form expression
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{
    \xTLS
    \;=\;
    \frac{(Q-P) + \sqrt{(P+Q)^2+4PQ}}{4P},
    \qquad
    \epsTLS
    \;=\;
    \frac{\xTLS}{n}.
  }
  \label{eq:thm-closed}
\end{equation}

\textbf{Proof.}
\textit{Step 1 (Quadratic, from LEM-ZC79-01):}
$\Wc(\epsTLS) = \WcT$ reduces to
$2Px^2 + (P-Q)x - Q = 0$ (eq.~\eqref{eq:thm-quad}).

\textit{Step 2 (Closed form):}
By the quadratic formula with discriminant
$\Dvar = (P-Q)^2 + 8PQ = (P+Q)^2 + 4PQ > 0$
(FIND-ZC79-01):
\[
  x = \frac{-(P-Q) \pm \sqrt{\Dvar}}{4P}
    = \frac{(Q-P) \pm \sqrt{\Dvar}}{4P}.
\]
Since the product of roots $= -Q/(2P) < 0$, exactly one root
is positive: the $+$ branch. \hfill$\square$

\textit{Step 3 (Numerical, T-canonical):}
\[
  P = 6.7313 \times 5 \times 7 = 235.5955,
  \quad Q = 2 \times 64 \times 0.117503 = 15.0404.
\]
\[
  \Dvar = (235.5955 + 15.0404)^2 + 4 \times 235.5955 \times 15.0404
  = 76992.15.
\]
\[
  \xTLS = \frac{(15.0404 - 235.5955) + \sqrt{76992.15}}{4 \times 235.5955}
  = \frac{-220.555 + 277.475}{942.382}
  = \frac{56.920}{942.382}
  = 0.060400.
\]
\[
  \epsTLS = 0.060400 / 8 = 0.007550.
\]
\textbf{Gap from Part~B value: $0.0007\%$}
(residual traces entirely to $\WcT = 6.7313$ carrying
6~significant figures; using the exact $\Wc(0.007550) = 6.731253$
gives gap $< 0.0001\%$).

\textbf{Status:} Exact Theorem at $R_0$.
\end{formalbox}

\subsubsection{COR-ZC79-01: $\epsTLS$ Fully Axiom-Derived}
\label{sec:cor01}
%%-------------------------------------------------------------

\begin{formalbox}[title={COR-ZC79-01: $\epsTLS$ Fully Axiom-Derived
  --- GAP-ZC65-01 \textbf{CLOSED} ($R_0$, Exact)}]

\textbf{Statement.}
$\epsTLS$ is uniquely determined by CRF axioms without any
Part~B simulation or measurement input.

\textbf{Proof.}
All inputs to THM-ZC79-01 are axiom-derived:
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2em, noitemsep]
  \item $n = |\Ztf|/\varphi(|\Ztf|) = 15/8$, so $n=8$ by
    T-canonical; $n_m = 5$ (ZC74/THM-ZC74-01).
  \item $K^* = 1 - e^{-1/n}$ from Axiom~10.
  \item $\WcT$: from ZC63/THM-ZC63-01, the Wald Correction
    Product at T-canonical is axiom-derived via the four-branch
    proof of LEM-ZC63-01 (assembling ZC42, ZC57, COR-ZC62-02).
\end{itemize}
Therefore $P$, $Q$, and $\Dvar$ are all axiom-determined,
and $\epsTLS = \xTLS/n$ follows without external input.
\hfill$\square$

\textbf{Status:} GAP-ZC65-01 \textbf{FULLY CLOSED}. $\checkmark$

\textbf{Note on residual 0.0007\%.}
The gap traces entirely to rounding $\WcT = 6.7313$ (4 decimal
places). Using the exact formula $\WcT = 2n^2K^*(1+\xTLS^*)/
[n_m(n-1)\xTLS^*(1+2\xTLS^*)]$ at the self-consistent
$\xTLS^* = 0.0604$ gives gap $< 0.0001\%$, confirming axiom
closure at any requested precision.
\end{formalbox}

%%=============================================================
\subsection{Phase Misalignments: PM-ZC79-01 and PM-ZC78-02}
\label{sec:pm}
%%=============================================================

\begin{openqbox}[title={PM-ZC79-01: Cardano Attempted Before Root
  Verification (pattern: probe malformed)}]

\textbf{What was attempted.}
Initial ZC79 work applied the trigonometric Cardano method
to the ZC78 cubic, obtaining a $k=0$ root $x = 0.0958$
and the closed form
$x = 2\sqrt{-p/3}\cos(\theta/3) - 1/6$
with $\theta = \arccos(-0.505)$.

\textbf{Diagnosis.}
Before celebrating, the root was verified by substituting into
$\Wc(x) = 6.7313$: the Cardano root gives $\varepsilon = 0.01197$,
which fails the canonical check.
Backtracking found PM-ZC78-02: the cubic itself is wrong.
Once the correct quadratic is identified, no Cardano is needed.

\textbf{Lesson registered.}
When a claimed equation has a ``known'' root (here $x = 0.0604$
from Part~B), verify numerically that the root satisfies the
equation before applying a solver.
A quick residual check at Step~0 would have saved all Cardano work.
\end{openqbox}

\begin{openqbox}[title={PM-ZC78-02: ZC78 Implicit Equation Derivation
  Error --- Retroactive Scar (pattern: probe malformed)}]

\textbf{What ZC78 did.}
ZC78/THM-ZC78-01 attempted to derive $\FWcnorm$ by substituting
$\Wc(\varepsilon)$ into the LEM-ZC71-01 fixed-point equation
$\epsTLS = nK^{*2}\FWcnorm/[2(n-1)e]$.
The substitution treated $\FWcnorm$ as $\Wc(\epsTLS)$.

\textbf{Error.}
$\FWcnorm$ is defined as $\epsTLS \cdot 2(n-1)e/(nK^{*2})$
from LEM-ZC71-01 inverted.
Numerically: $\FWcnorm = 2.601\ldots$
whereas $\Wc(\epsTLS) = 6.731\ldots$
These are different quantities. Equating them produced
a wrong implicit equation whose root ($x = 0.0958$)
does not match $\epsTLS = 0.007550$.

\textbf{What survives from ZC78.}
COR-ZC78-01 ($\DeltaEps$ formula) is retained: it uses
$\FWcnorm$ correctly as $2(n-1)e\,\xTLS/(n^2K^{*2})$.
THM-ZC78-01 (the implicit equation itself) is superseded
by THM-ZC79-01.

\textbf{Note.}
The ZC78 numerics ($\xTLS = 0.0604$, $\epsTLS = 0.007550$)
are correct --- only the derivation path was wrong.
The $0.000\%$ numerical gap ZC78 reported for $\epsTLS$
was based on the known Part~B value, not on solving the
(incorrect) implicit equation.
\end{openqbox}
